      1. Barbara Lindqvist
      2. Walter Brennan
      3. Louisa Lindqvist
      4. Eula Selvi
      5. Jordan Novak
      6. Marguerite Bennett

      The grid loads lazily, and its filter only matches names. So I went through the surnames one at a time, 23 in all, and checked each result. I also sorted by last name in both directions to confirm no surname sorts before Bennett or after Tesi. I haven't directly checked surnames that fall between those two ends and weren't among the 23. That seems unlikely, since the data reuses the same small set of surnames, but I can't rule it out.
